# fc88638 Merge pull request #6 from steven91007/mcp-submodule

## Summary
Merges PR #6 into master: the MCP server moves to the steven91007/jobseekers-mcp repository and is mounted as a git submodule at jobseekers-mcp/, .mcp.json installs it in editable mode, requirements.txt drops mcp, tests/test_mcp.py runs the submodule's checks against this checkout, and gitkb notes are added for PRs #2-#5 and the move. Relative to master this is 4bc8390 plus 1b93461.

## Why
Lands the separately maintained MCP server on master.

## Risks
Existing clones need `git submodule update --init` before .mcp.json can start the server; the server depends on this repo's module interfaces across a repository boundary.

## Files
- `.gitmodules` (A, +3/-0): Registers the jobseekers-mcp submodule at path jobseekers-mcp. [note](../changes/edb75d646107a5a5209017dc8c46dd7df7c8fb78e52fe13ca76ac1098b095ece.md)
- `.mcp.json` (M, +1/-1): Starts the server with uv run --with-editable ./jobseekers-mcp instead of --with-requirements requirements.txt. [note](../changes/6d1b4014c74a0be76237fd97bbd098a3905093eb17c81ff3d456e5e45b2d036a.md)
- `README.md` (M, +29/-23): The MCP section now points to the jobseekers-mcp repository and covers cloning with submodules, the launch and --check commands, and how to bump the submodule; test docs updated. [note](../changes/fe5d0f3084ded1ce6b33a9fa87d7fed90c4b122df1ff48d3d5ece07aab4965ac.md)
- `jobseekers-mcp` (A, +1/-0): Submodule pointer to steven91007/jobseekers-mcp (the extracted MCP server package). [note](../changes/9c163063f77db42cdbef52c63939708162df197d5a6715ef1985ee24710b63c9.md)
